Existing required vectors also cover: V1 pair absent and explicit null; partial pair and V2 mismatch; alias source version 2 under metadata version 1; exact original kebab grammar including repeated interior hyphen; non-NFC accepted historical strings and differing timestamp offsets; invalid UTF-8, ordinal/case-insensitive recursive duplicate names (including non-ASCII), unknown V1 member renderer versus V2 rejection and null/empty maps; protected/provider-opaque raw preservation; per-hop schema/identity and deterministic output; max version/reachability/16-hop boundary; 64 MiB zero-hop V1 and each output/result/page/array/timeline/metadata ceiling; 100,000 max-metadata array failure; sequence/global overflow; route/prefix/empty-prefix/paged continuation replay and lost-response exact retry, proof-container and checkpoint-anchor exact bytes, separate retry blob capacity failure after computation, lost incomplete/final response and crash recovery; forged DTO/effective bytes and changed CloudEvent id/type/source/topic/header; JSON structured/binary equivalent decoded body and strict Base64; old queued flat-JSON delivery/new binary ingress, pre-commit legacy handoff/outbox ack and crash/redelivery race, marker/snapshot/checkpoint/key obligations, purpose-06 encoding sidecar, snapshot accumulator seed/update, delete absence receipt, per-output F rejection and case-variant protection extension hold; production legacy-handler receipt hold, concurrent duplicate effects and global cross-tenant ID collision; rollback with V2 history and V1-only endpoint fencing; cancellation at every §9 boundary; state-store end state, checkpoint readback and full-replay equivalence. Diagnostics assert reason codes, sequence, size, version and hop count only, without payload or secrets.

Review-33 targeted production vectors cover: a provider whose typed `EventStreamReader` succeeds but bounded raw-source transport/proof fails; reject reader activation, and verify exact original raw bytes, sidecar, head/ETag and tamper/oversize behavior before normalization. Exercise F at 64 MiB readable output and just above it, 128 MiB whole-result and scratch boundaries, wrong declared alias/version/format, missing/second Complete, cancellation and post-seal mutation; no full output allocation or append occurs on failure. For an incomplete paged replay, assert every `AggregateReconstructionResult` field above, the single pinned token, old-endpoint rejection, lost response exact retry and final-only `Succeeded`/state/proof. Forge, copy across principals, expire and revoke a named read capability; each must deny before key/root lookup and reveal no existence. Seed an overlapping legacy physical key, shared-generation row and marker under a proposed prefix; prove inventory, fenced copy/readback or nonoverlapping prefix selection and an old coordinator path before route activation. Fail backup export after an earlier valid page and after staging commit acknowledgement loss; no bytes/URL escape until atomic pointer/readback proves the same fixed-head artifact. Deliver authenticated Binary and structured `data_base64` dead letters at the old 1 MiB boundary and admitted 128 MiB boundary, malformed attestation, durable-capture failure, lost capture acknowledgement and no-ack-without-retention; compare exact body/header hashes and retained blob/index bytes. Preserve every signed V17/V20/V22/V23 literal and six-field UNAPPROVED receipt unchanged.


Review-32 targeted production vectors cover: one custom upcaster that tries `MemoryMarshal.TryGetMemoryManager`, `TryGetArray`, Pin, owner downcast and retained-facade reads, plus mutation of a caller-owned copy; none exposes or changes the private source, and cancellation/failed-hop digest checks still precede any next hop or Apply. Prepare two commands with one original command MessageId in different tenant/domain/aggregate scopes: their separately encoded preparation CAS keys and batch roots differ, while a same-scope changed input conflicts and generated event MessageIds remain globally unique. Execute a named batch whose operations have no OperationId member: every logical-witness tag `03` and root tag `07` uses its enclosing `ReadModelBatchScope.BatchId`; changing BatchId, store, tenant, domain, aggregate, projection, route/key-space, ordinal or source intent changes the production batch/root fingerprint and cannot be an exact retry. Let a scheduled TTL job retain old root R while an unrelated commit advances to R2: if the same expiring leaf persists, reconcile any ambiguous R attempt and CAS-link a successor derived from R2, then commit exactly one tombstone; a replaced or absent leaf supersedes the old job without deleting the new value. Admit 256 valid large claims to stream, Admin, trace and backup readers separately: premeasure, shrink and re-sign each bounded contiguous page, then continue without a gap or partial export; one overlimit event returns `ProofLimit` once. Let authoritative UTC cross a selected leaf's expiry after initial proof while the root pointer stays unchanged: final fenced recheck returns `ReadModelExpiryPending`, never stale bytes; test separate-lease renewal and a provider whose renewal changes ETag, requiring a new certified root before retention lapses or readiness hold. Review-22 targeted production vectors cover: a two-page command whose first effective payload changes while the final page and stored accumulator remain fixed (purpose-05 chain, ledger transcript and purpose-07 terminal hash must reject it); final proof framing/Base64 immediately below, at and above the outer 2 MiB cap before final Apply; V1 unknown-member `-0` versus `0` and escaped versus literal string with equal canonical StoredDigest and identical decoder/validator decisions; handler-only transitive managed/native dependency drift changing RegistryFingerprint and selected HandlerCompatibilityHash while leaving an unrelated EventTransformHash unchanged; stale generation racing a two-store `TryWriteWithFence` after coordinator advance; legacy handoff repeated under changed broker attempt ID and conflicting source evidence; forged, expired or wrong-revision membership, consumer lease and broker publication-fence claims, plus a membership advance racing `TryPublishAtRevision`; a lone wrong-case V2 known property and a case-insensitive duplicate pair, while V1 historical casing remains admitted; exact-size and rounded-pool scratch owners at the 128 MiB boundary; and unknown/wrong-typed compile-time option names with the seven-name V17 fixture unchanged. Review-21 targeted production vectors additionally cover: a lost acknowledgement on each of response-blob creation, final-result creation and ledger-pointer CAS, including an orphan prepared blob, a committed pointer with a lost response, and an inconclusive three-object readback that retains quota and forbids second Apply; old immutable checkpoint state surviving a new state write, a failed pointer CAS and two-store fence race, plus same-store atomic update; exact encoded final response at, below and above 128 MiB with Base64/JSON escaping and optional timeline, fresh authenticated state-only replay and expired 15-minute token with a still-valid 24-hour final pin; purpose-06 sidecar absence before reader activation, a live old V1 writer racing cutover, upgraded V1 append atomic sidecar/readback, and dormant reader deployment; one authenticated component/topic/route/MessageId handoff redelivered under a different broker attempt ID with changed source digest/route/pin; old purpose-02 pin under an approved prior fingerprint re-evolved under a current purpose-01 route; verified-only projection handler discovery and old/new overlap on every live/retry/history/rebuild path; explicit canonical subscription registration and duplicate/effect-provider readiness; aggregate replay rejecting a valid projection-only checkpoint anchor; external subscriber dedup/receipt lease failure, shared-topic one-pin send, and broker duplicate after JSON handoff. All assertions inspect durable state, bytes, pointers and acknowledgements, not only status codes.

Terminal boundary known-answer **field-core** vector (framing, not a standalone signed proof): prefix tags `05..09` for `start=end=head=target=long.MaxValue` and `count=0` are exactly `057fffffffffffffff067fffffffffffffff077fffffffffffffff087fffffffffffffff0900000000` under §4 N/I. It is accepted only with zero route count `00000000`, matching cumulative accumulator and the signed witnessed snapshot (`0d` absent, `0e`/`0f` present) or projection checkpoint (`0d` present with matching container entry and state pointer, `0e`/`0f` absent). The aggregate `/replay-state/pages` endpoint rejects the checkpoint variant. For **this count-zero sentinel**, `count=1` or any route entry fails before Apply; a normal count-one page with a verified route at sequence `long.MaxValue`, prior accumulator through `long.MaxValue-1`, and head/target at max is valid and completes once. Any next token or attempted `long.MaxValue+1` fails before arithmetic. The literal count-zero core above is unchanged. The ordinary k=`long.MaxValue-1` zero-tail still encodes start=`long.MaxValue`, end=`long.MaxValue-1`, count 0. The literal core was generated with Python signed big-endian `to_bytes(8)` and independently recomputed by the embedded Node verifier below.

Rejected: a named progress leaf hashing its own newly signed checkpoint, retrospectively sorting shared-key updates by per-stream sequence/aggregate ID, treating present-key `IdempotentAbsent` delete as conflict, omitting `ValueTypeName` from a logical retry witness, changing key-space ownership without migration, advancing source sequence for TTL expiry, leaving `SharedProjectionEpochCoordinator` on unguarded generic physical-key writes, materializing an unbounded backup export, deriving optional CloudEvent subject/time from mutable current defaults, returning an upcaster-owned output alias from `Complete`, or admitting a zero-row transform primitive as production evidence; volatile append regeneration on exact retry; independent per-ID Committed transitions for one actor batch; recomputing old StoredDigest under a current parser; accepting an old handler whose generic type cannot implement its selected callable effect contract; arbitrary effect-capable DI sinks outside the transaction/provider; counting the 64 MiB page and proof limits as separate additions to the 128 MiB live scratch budget; public replay records that expose internal types or raw mutable scratch; in-place named-projection rows visible before checkpoint pointer commit; friendly state-store name as backend identity; signed timeline chunks without an implemented/proved protocol; mutable publisher configuration as committed route authority; raw event bytes as proof of historic encoding ID; a multi-type handler inferred from its primary type alone; one lease for several logical handlers; partial-batch MessageId retry acceptance; plaintext or unmanaged spool, unzeroed cross-tenant pooled scratch, unprotected/unscoped response blobs, declared-only external dedup provider, precheck-only lease expiry, new nonce after uncertain broker acceptance, cross-mode raw-byte equality requirement, hashing unexpanded option defaults; broker-attempt ID as a stable handoff key; unfenced cross-store generation reads; case-insensitive V2 spelling admission; a final-page-only effective proof for multi-page commands; a caller-controlled consumer lease; unreserved pool rounding; runtime edits in Story 6.5; CLR names as public identity or arbitrary type loading; generic V1 version default; ordinary history rewrite; upcast at publication followed by another at subscription; DTO-only attestation or generic object publication; local/tenant-only MessageId uniqueness; standalone effect reservation; skip/ack past projection poison; unbounded whole-array replay/`GetEventsAsync(0)`; treating a page/token as complete history; trusting a free snapshot object; key expiry as blanket verification permission; AOT/trimming assertion; Epic 8 as prerequisite. **Open decisions: none.** Any newly discovered incompatible historical/provider fact blocks readiness and requires an explicitly approved amendment or offline migration, not ad hoc 6.6 behavior.

Fixed known-answer **codec framing** vectors follow. These small records test
exact bytes and hash framing; zero-field registry, transform and delivery
records are deliberately semantically inadmissible and MUST still fail
production validation. Python `hashlib` and Node.js `crypto` independently
recomputed every SHA-256 value; Python `cryptography` produced the fixed
signature and Node.js `crypto.verify` independently accepted it. Story 6.6
also tests full admitted event fixtures listed above.

| Vector | Literal input / expected bytes or hash |
| --- | --- |
| Primitive presence and maps | `O(M)` absent `00`, explicit null `01`, present empty `0200000000`; first bitmap position value `0200000000`, null `0100000000`; nested protection position (index 19) value `0000000080`, null `0000000040`. Raw `eventstore.protection` selector for JSON string `"a"` and escaped `"\u0061"` is identically `020000000161`; empty string is `0200000000`, null `01`, absent `00`; Unicode normalization is not performed. |
| Raw renderer ordering | JSON input bytes UTF-8 `{"b":null,"a":1}` and `{ "a":1, "\u0062":null }` with all-zero framing bitmap both yield canonical bytes `48582d45562d5241572d310001050000000000060000000201000000016103000000013101000000016200`; ordinal/case-insensitive duplicate `{ "a":0,"A":1 }` fails. The all-zero bitmap is a framing fixture, not valid event presence. |
| Original timestamp offset | `T` for UTC instant `2026-01-01T00:00:00Z` is `08de48c8b4f800000000`; the same instant stored as `2026-01-01T01:00:00+01:00` is `08de48c8b4f80000003c`; `Q` for both is `08de48c8b4f80000`. |
| Protection record | Exact bytes `48582d45562d50524f54454354494f4e2d3100010006010000000b556e70726f74656374656402000000010301040105010600`; SHA-256 `692bb3c5ac845633f2a8b30b5862c3b32ebe5f4bd0039faca7daeef79d14f2be`. |
| Missing-extension legacy protection record | Exact bytes `48582d45562d50524f54454354494f4e2d3100010006010000000b556e70726f746563746564020000000103010401050106020000000101000000066c65676163790201000000076d697373696e67`; SHA-256 `01455b45110717676f918741a282b8051a053abcba579b5c5243f9422b269f59`. |
| StoredDigest | `SHA256("HX-EV-STORED-1\0" || 01 || B(raw renderer bytes above) || 00 absent raw protection || U("json") || B32(protection hash above))` = `793c9ab1614593b2126d8fa7075b545359166ba57e02a9f52b3b2c4a0298d660`. |
| DeliveryDigest | Canonical zero-field body bytes `48582d45562d44454c49564552592d424f44592d3100010000`; `SHA256("HX-EV-DELIVERY-1\0" || 01 || B(body))` = `44fe4edfeec7d524453f62645832206269f332b70427c8af30a1647a2fd6dd32`. |
| RegistryFingerprint | `SHA256("HX-EV-REGISTRY-1\0" || 01 || u32 zero)` = `c9fb290b9cbbf614974e36da7514249399c801e7956c9b30219964ed0785706f`. |
| EventTransformHash primitive (inadmissible as a production transform) | `SHA256("HX-EV-TRANSFORM-1\0" || 01 || U("d") || U("a") || u32 zero || B32 zero)` = `e10cf7257370fc6d7958412e0032880aa3e881ebca2c49a762dae0c173a6e233`. This checks only the hash-prefix arithmetic; a production transform must contain the required D/V/A/E, S-read and protection-adapter rows and a valid row count. |
| StateCheckpointReadbackHash | `SHA256("HX-EV-STATE-READBACK-1\0" || 01 || U("t") || U("d") || U("s") || U("k") || U("r") || N(1) || U("o") || U("e") || U("j") || H(32 zero bytes) || B(empty))` = `0073d1af128b962eb847c122deb381a0f9a2ad3fb2b71f579280ee3527377c2c`. |

explicitly registered as an alias before a production reader accepts it.

Review-33 rejected: treating typed `EventStreamReader` objects or current-provider configuration as original raw/encoding evidence; F returning a fully allocated array for late size validation; marking an incomplete page `Succeeded` or `Partial`; accepting a constructed `NamedProjectionReadScope` as authorization; enabling a new prefix guard over unidentified legacy physical keys; releasing backup HTTP chunks before final fixed-head validation and atomic completion; and acknowledging an uncaptured dead letter because a finite broker retry budget has ended. Each would bypass a durable authority or boundary required above. These are closed design choices, not 6.6 discretion.

Review-35 targeted production vectors complete the last corrections: verify a Slice-1 upgraded V1 append stages exact event, purpose-06 encoding, version-2 StoredDigest, origin branch-01, acyclic pre-save intent certificate, outbox and domain result in one atomic actor save and verifies the separate provider commit receipt; retained V1 branch-02 works only with independent offline evidence. Tamper each of the four `AuthenticatedRawEvent` byte records, event key, provider namespace/encoding, backend, generation, head/ETag, fence and raw-readback signature before typed normalization; a caller-built proof and stale head fail. Exercise the public scratch callback with a retained callback/lease attempt, cancellation and pool reuse across tenants; no `Memory`/owner/pointer escapes, and full capacity is zero before quota release. Grant 256 exact 512-byte keys through the 256 KiB authenticated key-set blob, tamper one key/hash/count, saturate quota and race record/blob cleanup; no broadened prefix or partial read is allowed. For paged replay compare null page-1 continuation to present-empty and to a later exact signed token; reject every caller effective/adaptation/verified-view member before fingerprint and pinned-response lookup. Compare tag-12 outbox B lengths and hashes for both discriminator variants, vary only legal HTTP header-name case and then add case-fold duplicates/changed values. Mutate ConsumerConfiguration tag-`0b` effect mode under an unchanged `000f` lease and require hash/revision failure. Keep a Prepared capsule during Pending/Ready/ambiguous save and active retry, then publish and read back the compact completion record after all obligations close; reclaim chunks and quota only after authenticated deletion, and make an exact late retry return the original result without volatile regeneration. Preserve every V17/V20/V22/V23 literal input, hash, signature and the six-field UNAPPROVED receipt; inspect durable bytes and state, not just statuses.

Review-36 production vectors close the last timing, evidence and retention gaps. In Slice 1, commit a bounded V1 event with event/encoding/digest/outbox/result, pre-save intent certificate and branch-01 origin in **one** actor save; issue purpose-11 only afterward, verify its record list includes every staged record and excludes itself, then tamper certificate, origin, receipt, backend, generation, key interval and raw-source record hashes before V1 admission. Read branch-02 retained evidence through the authenticated source lookup. Rotate purpose-11 verifier-first and retain the original key/implementation for an old V1 delivery, backup and rollback. Prepare a named root/checkpoint with only a prepared-record ETag, lose the preparation acknowledgement, then publish through one final pointer CAS that produces the actual pointer ETag/visibility receipt; lose that acknowledgement too and reconcile by OperationId. Before receipt readback, queries see only the prior certified root. Handoff one authenticated old JSON body to a two-route binary subscription, vary whitespace with equal decoded fields, then change one JSON payload/protection/header byte, route, original side record, binary pin or send intent; only the fully authenticated original may be acknowledged. Change one field of the bounded `EventEffectReceipt` and require `ReceiptDigest`, V3 tags `13`/`14` and route duplicate lookup to disagree before effect/ack. Compact a no-op capsule only after its result/checkpoint, intent, receipt and obligations verify, then exact-retry after chunk reclamation without an event or new volatile value. Independently saturate the 128 MiB capability index and the 1 GiB/100,000 grant-blob quota, race token/blob deletion, and prove neither charge is released on ambiguous readback. Lose a paged response and restart the gateway: resend the exact previously pinned signed request bytes or return `ReplayRestartRequired` and begin fresh page 1; a newly signed equivalent old-operation request must fail exact retry. For an irrecoverably corrupt retained raw item, prove actor key/head/provider bytes, AD-31 capture and authenticated operator decision before recording `RawCorruptDisposition`; replay still stops at that sequence with last-good state and no fabricated digest/origin/checkpoint or silent ack. Preserve all V17/V20/V22/V23 literal signed fixture bytes and the six-field UNAPPROVED receipt; assert durable records, bytes, state, route effects and broker acknowledgements rather than status alone.

### 11.1 Admitted one-event V1 and signed interoperability fixture

Unlike the zero-record framing vectors above, this fixture has a registered V1 alias and an active complete one-event route. The raw V1 source has valid ULID-shaped MessageId, AggregateId and correlation/causation IDs, stored metadata version 1, absent canonical pair and null extensions. The exact raw JSON bytes are V17SourceJsonUtf8Hex below; its payload base64 is e30= (UTF-8 {}), provider encoding ID is json, and raw protection extension is absent. The exact alias is (d, Legacy.Event) -> (evt, source version 1, json), with no payload identity claim under its registered NoPayloadIdentity policy; the addressed envelope still binds tenant t, domain d, aggregate type x and aggregate ID. D current version is 1, so this is a verified zero-hop fixture. Handler route (d, route) consumes evt on d-events and uses AtomicStore. Active RegistryFingerprint is V17RegistryHash. Test key test-key is trusted for purposes 01 through 04 and 06 over UTC ticks 630822816000000000 through 662380416000000000. The key is the fixed P-256 public SPKI already shown above, with fixture private scalar 1 used only to generate signatures. Purpose-02, purpose-04 and purpose-06 claims sign issuance UTC `2026-01-01T00:00:00Z` (Q bytes `08de48c8b4f80000`), within that key interval. The checkpoint uses coordinator `fixture-fence`, generation 1, state operation `op` and state version `etag1` under the shared fence. The source route and prefix fixture does not exercise a command processor, so it has no purpose-07 key or command-state proof. The fixture implementation-file bytes are ASCII fixture-assembly followed by LF; every assembly H below is their SHA-256. The fixture options manifest is the exact sorted UTF-8 JSON represented by V17OptionsTextUtf8Hex, including converter, reader, serializer, signer and validator settings; every options H is the domain-separated hash of V17OptionsCanonical below. Test descriptors D/V/A/S and route R use the exact §5 field tables with those H values and the following IDs: aggregate route r, current CLR type Fixture.Event, Fixture, Apply apply, state schema 1 bytes {}, serializer j, V validator schema, V identity no-payload-identity, A validator alias-schema and decoder noop; S component IDs raw, chain, json, reader, protection, verifier, signer, publisher, reservation, renderer; route handler, effect and readback IDs handler, effect and readback. No E or F row is needed. All raw, manifest, transform and claim encodings are literal, not placeholders.

The fixture proof container is a complete replay from sequence 1 with one route entry, one prefix entry and checkpoint discriminator `00`; its prefix checkpoint-anchor field remains absent. `V17CheckpointAnchorHash` separately exercises the exact checkpoint-entry hash for a later projection tail; this one-event replay does not claim that tail path. The encoding sidecar is signed with purpose 06 and checked against the same raw JSON bytes before the V1 StoredDigest is admitted.

The named byte values below are lowercase hex. They are the exact inputs for §4–§7 hashing and signing; the byte lengths of RegistryBytes, TransformBytes, RouteClaim, PrefixClaim, CheckpointClaim, DeliveryBody and DeliveryClaim are respectively 1723, 1503, 295, 213, 351, 842 and 223. The batch row witnesses one present (`01`) upsert of canonical readback bytes {} at (store, key), sequence 1, operation op, resulting version etag1. The purpose-06 sidecar binds the exact V1 raw bytes to provider encoding ID json and namespace dapr-state at the actor event key; its key is present in the TrustMapDigest. The checkpoint claim binds its BatchStateReadbackHash, signed issuance UTC and shared fence identity/generation/state operation/version; a missing row or stale fence would reject completion. Delivery extensions contain the exact readable restamp of the absent stored protection extension, with legacy=missing. V1 routing headers derive evt and 1 from the alias while the signed delivery body keeps both stored pair fields absent. The attestation extension is the exact hyevattestation Binary bytes; its padded RFC 4648 Base64 representation is included for structured/binary transport mapping.

Expected values are also literal. The four route/prefix/checkpoint/delivery claim signatures and the encoding sidecar signature are fixed 64-byte IEEE P1363 values; a verifier must reject a changed claim byte, wrong purpose, wrong scope or stale RegistryFingerprint.

Reproduction: decode the SourceJsonUtf8Hex, render and bitmap it under §4, and compare CanonicalRaw and StoredDigest. Decode RegistryBytes, TransformBytes, HandlerRow and the four claims with the fixed field tables; reject wrong counts, tags, order, options or alias. Recompute each listed SHA-256 from its literal input bytes. Parse Spki as P-256 SPKI and verify each signature over the §6 signature input using test-key and purposes route 01, prefix 03, checkpoint 04, delivery 02, encoding evidence 06. Decode ProofContainer under §6 and compute CheckpointAnchorHash from the exact signed checkpoint entry. Check the exact one-event prefix list and accumulator against StoredDigest, the batch row hash against the checkpoint claim, and the decoded Binary attestation against DeliveryClaim and DeliverySignature. Python hashlib/cryptography generated these values; independent Node.js crypto code rerendered SourceJsonUtf8Hex, recomputed StoredDigest and the listed byte hashes, verified all five fixed P1363 signatures and rejected one-byte tampering. Both runs passed for this revision. A production verifier additionally checks the active registry/trust, current key, provider readback, admission limits and route/effect readiness; a framing-only zero-row vector above never passes those checks.


Run this independent verification from the repository root (Node.js with P-256 support):

```bash
node <<'JS'
const fs = require('node:fs');
const crypto = require('node:crypto');
const doc = fs.readFileSync('_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-event-versioning-upcasting.md', 'utf8');
const special = {raw_json:'SourceJsonUtf8Hex', canonical_raw:'CanonicalRaw', options_text:'OptionsTextUtf8Hex', attestation:'AttestationExtension', sread_row:'SReadRow'};
const keys = 'raw_json canonical_raw bitmap options_text options_canonical trust_bytes alias_row descriptor_row version_row shared_row sread_row protection_adapter_row registry_bytes transform_bytes handler_row route_claim prefix_claim batch_row checkpoint_body checkpoint_claim encoding_claim encoding_signature proof_container checkpoint_anchor_hash readable_extensions routing_headers delivery_body delivery_claim attestation spki assembly_hash options_hash trust_hash state_hash registry_hash transform_hash handler_hash stored_digest consumed_hash route_claim_hash route_signature list_hash genesis accumulator prefix_claim_hash prefix_signature batch_hash checkpoint_body_hash checkpoint_claim_hash checkpoint_signature delivery_digest delivery_claim_hash delivery_signature'.split(' ');
const label = k => special[k] || k.split('_').map(x=>x[0].toUpperCase()+x.slice(1)).join('');
const v = Object.fromEntries(keys.map(k => {
 const re = new RegExp('^V17'+label(k)+'=([0-9a-f]+)$','gm');
 const found=[...doc.matchAll(re)];
 if(found.length!==1) throw Error('missing/duplicate '+label(k));
 return [k,found[0][1]];
}));
const B = x => Buffer.from(x, 'hex');
const H = x => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(x).digest('hex');
const U = s => { const b=Buffer.from(s,'utf8'); const n=Buffer.alloc(4); n.writeUInt32BE(b.length); return Buffer.concat([n,b]); };
const W = (...parts) => Buffer.concat(parts);
const chk = (name, value) => { if (value !== v[name]) throw Error(name + ': expected ' + v[name] + ', got ' + value); };
const render = x => {
 if (x===null) return Buffer.from([0]);
 if (x===false) return Buffer.from([1]);
 if (x===true) return Buffer.from([2]);
 if (typeof x==='number') return W(Buffer.from([3]),U(String(x)));
 if (typeof x==='string') return W(Buffer.from([4]),U(x));
 if (Array.isArray(x)) { const n=Buffer.alloc(4); n.writeUInt32BE(x.length); return W(Buffer.from([5]),n,...x.map(render)); }
 const keys=Object.keys(x).sort((a,b)=>Buffer.compare(Buffer.from(a),Buffer.from(b)));
 const n=Buffer.alloc(4); n.writeUInt32BE(keys.length);
 return W(Buffer.from([6]),n,...keys.map(k=>W(Buffer.from([1]),U(k),render(x[k]))));
};
const source=JSON.parse(B(v.raw_json).toString('utf8'));
const raw=W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-RAW-1\0\x01\x05'),B(v.bitmap),render(source));
const optsText=B(v.options_text).toString('utf8');
const opts=JSON.parse(optsText);
if(JSON.stringify(opts)+'\n'!==optsText || render(opts).toString('hex')!==v.options_canonical) throw Error('canonical options manifest');
if (raw.toString('hex')!==v.canonical_raw) throw Error('canonical V1 raw bytes');
chk('stored_digest',H(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-STORED-1\0\x01'),U(raw),Buffer.from([0]),U('json'),B('01455b45110717676f918741a282b8051a053abcba579b5c5243f9422b269f59'))));
chk('assembly_hash', H(Buffer.from('fixture-assembly\n')));
chk('options_hash', H(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-OPTIONS-1\0\x01'), U(B(v.options_canonical)))));
chk('trust_hash', H(B(v.trust_bytes)));
if (!B(v.trust_bytes).subarray(0,19).equals(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-TRUST-1\0\x01'),Buffer.from([0,0,0,5])))) throw Error('trust purpose count');
if (!B(v.trust_bytes).includes(B('4b00000001640600000008746573742d6b6579'))) throw Error('purpose-06 trust row');
const registryExpected=W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-REGISTRY-1\0\x01'),Buffer.from([0,0,0,4]),B(v.alias_row),B(v.descriptor_row),B(v.shared_row),B(v.version_row));
if(!B(v.registry_bytes).equals(registryExpected)) throw Error('registry row order/bytes');
const transformExpected=W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-TRANSFORM-1\0\x01'),U('d'),U('r'),Buffer.from([0,0,0,5]),B(v.alias_row),B(v.descriptor_row),B(v.version_row),B(v.state_hash),B(v.sread_row),B(v.protection_adapter_row));
if(!B(v.transform_bytes).equals(transformExpected)) throw Error('transform row order/bytes');
const parseRow = (rawBytes, expectedTag, pkTypes, fieldTypes, hasCodec = false) => {
 let at=0; const take=n=>{if(n<0 || at+n>rawBytes.length) throw Error('row truncated'); const out=rawBytes.subarray(at,at+n);at+=n;return out;};
 const byte=()=>take(1)[0];
 const scalar=t=>{
  if(t==='H') return take(32);
  if(t==='I') return take(4);
  if(t==='U' || t==='B') {const n=take(4).readUInt32BE(0);const value=take(n);if(t==='U' && !Buffer.from(new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(value)).equals(value)) throw Error('row UTF-8');return value;}
  if(t==='O') {const d=byte();if(d===2) return scalar('U');if(d>2) throw Error('row option');return null;}
  throw Error('row scalar type '+t);
 };
 if(byte()!==expectedTag || (hasCodec && byte()!==1)) throw Error('row tag/codec');
 for(const t of pkTypes) scalar(t);
 if(take(2).readUInt16BE(0)!==fieldTypes.length) throw Error('row field count');
 fieldTypes.forEach((t,j)=>{if(byte()!==j+1) throw Error('row field tag/order');scalar(t);});
 if(at!==rawBytes.length) throw Error('row trailing bytes');
};
const DTypes='U I U H U H I B U H H H'.split(' '), VTypes='U H H U H H U U H H'.split(' ');
parseRow(B(v.alias_row),0x41,['U','U'],'U I U U H H U H H U H H U H H'.split(' '));
parseRow(B(v.descriptor_row),0x44,['U','U'],DTypes);
parseRow(B(v.version_row),0x56,['U','U','I'],VTypes);
parseRow(B(v.shared_row),0x53,['U','O'],['H',...Array(10).fill(['U','H','H']).flat()]);
parseRow(B(v.sread_row),0x73,[],Array(6).fill(['U','H','H']).flat(),true);
parseRow(B(v.protection_adapter_row),0x70,[],['U','H','H'],true);
const GTypes=['U','H','U']; // A nonempty selected G set uses row tag 47, U/U/U key and exact 01..03 fields.
if(B(v.registry_bytes).subarray('HX-EV-REGISTRY-1\0\x01'.length).readUInt32BE(0)!==4) throw Error('registry count');
if(B(v.transform_bytes).subarray('HX-EV-TRANSFORM-1\0\x01'.length+U('d').length+U('r').length).readUInt32BE(0)!==5) throw Error('transform count');
if(GTypes.length!==3) throw Error('G schema');
chk('registry_hash', H(B(v.registry_bytes)));
chk('transform_hash', H(B(v.transform_bytes)));
chk('handler_hash', H(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-HANDLER-ROUTE-1\0\x01'), B(v.handler_row), Buffer.alloc(4), Buffer.alloc(4))));
for (const name of ['route','prefix','checkpoint','delivery']) chk(name+'_claim_hash', H(B(v[name+'_claim'])));
chk('list_hash', H(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-PREFIX-LIST-1\0\x01'),Buffer.from([0,0,0,1]),Buffer.from('0000000000000001','hex'),B(v.stored_digest))));
chk('accumulator', H(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-CHECKPOINT-STEP-1\0'),B(v.genesis),Buffer.from('0000000000000001','hex'),B(v.stored_digest))));
chk('batch_hash', H(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-BATCH-READBACK-1\0\x01'),Buffer.from([0,0,0,1]),B(v.batch_row))));
chk('checkpoint_body_hash',H(B(v.checkpoint_body)));
chk('delivery_digest',H(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-DELIVERY-1\0\x01'),U(B(v.delivery_body)))));
const N = x => { const z=Buffer.alloc(8); z.writeBigInt64BE(BigInt(x)); return z; };
const I = x => { const z=Buffer.alloc(4); z.writeInt32BE(x); return z; };
const Opt = (x,enc=U) => x===null ? Buffer.from([0]) : W(Buffer.from([2]),enc(x));
const Count = x => { const z=Buffer.alloc(4); z.writeUInt32BE(x); return z; };
const C = (name,fields) => { const n=Buffer.alloc(2); n.writeUInt16BE(fields.length); return W(Buffer.from(name+'\0\x01'),n,...fields.map((f,j)=>W(Buffer.from([j+1]),f))); };
const Eq = (name,got) => { if(got.toString('hex')!==v[name]) throw Error(name+' encoding'); };
const mid=source.messageId, aid=source.aggregateId, cid=source.correlationId;
const ts=B('08de48c8b4f800000000'), stored=B(v.stored_digest), registry=B(v.registry_hash), acc=B(v.accumulator);
const consumed=H(W(Buffer.from('HX-EV-CONSUMED-1\0\x01'),N(0),ts,U(cid),Opt(cid),Opt('u'),Opt('1'),U('Legacy.Event'),U('json'),I(1),Opt(null),Opt(null,I),stored));
chk('consumed_hash',consumed);
Eq('encoding_claim',C('HX-EV-ENCODING-EVIDENCE-1',[U('t'),U('d'),U(aid),U('x'),U('t:d:'+aid+':events:1'),N(1),U('json'),B(H(B(v.raw_json))),U('dapr-state'),B('08de48c8b4f80000')]));
Eq('route_claim',C('HX-EV-ROUTE-1',[stored,U('t'),U('d'),U(aid),U('x'),N(1),U(mid),U('Legacy.Event'),I(1),Opt(null),Opt(null,I),U('json'),U('evt'),I(1),registry,B(H(Buffer.from('{}'))),U('json'),B(consumed)]));
Eq('prefix_claim',C('HX-EV-PREFIX-1',[U('t'),U('d'),U(aid),U('x'),N(1),N(1),N(1),N(1),I(1),B(v.list_hash),acc,registry,Opt(null),Opt(null),Opt(null)]));
```

### 11.2 Compact V2, one-hop and protected-source byte vectors

These are additional admitted evidence shapes, derived from the signed V17 fixture under the exact §4/§5/§6 codecs; the one-hop route is a separate registered test manifest and is not signed by V17's zero-hop registry key. `V20V2RawJson` is literal UTF-8 (no final newline); its `eventTypeName=evt`, `metadataVersion=2`, `eventContractType=evt` and `payloadVersion=1` form a valid V2 pair under V17's current-version-1 D/V descriptor. The V2 bitmap covers both value pair members while retaining the absent raw protection position. The one-hop manifest retains V17 A/S/V1 rows, changes D current version to 2, adds the literal V2 row and E row below, and uses zero G rows. E registers deterministic `fixture-default-n` with exact `json` input `{}` and output `{"n":1}`, a version-2 schema accepting that output, and the same explicit `NoPayloadIdentity` policy. The hop's route claim uses V17's immutable V1 StoredDigest/consumed metadata and addressed scope, target `(evt,2,json)`, this manifest fingerprint and SHA-256 of the exact output; `V20OneHopRouteClaimHash` is SHA-256 of its exact §6 claim bytes. A separate dependency-row vector appends one selected G row after the protection adapter, changing the row count from five to six and the transform hash; it exercises the placement rule without altering V17’s signed manifest. These values are independent of the signed V17 route, which correctly remains target version 1.

For protected-source evidence, start from the exact V17 JSON source and replace `extensions:null` with `extensions:{"eventstore.protection":"<scalar>"}` where `<scalar>` is the decoded UTF-8 string given below, serialized as one JSON string member. `V20Protected` uses a test registered bounded `fixture` protection adapter whose readable result is `{}`; the source's original scalar remains unchanged and is included in StoredDigest even after unprotect. `V20ProviderOpaque` uses an unsupported-state scalar; its derived version-1 `ProviderOpaque` record has null scheme/key alias/content hint and flags `reason=unsupported-state`, while the source scalar remains unchanged. ProviderOpaque is source evidence and a typed read hold, never a fabricated readable payload or Apply. Both use V17's exact original raw body fields, provider encoding ID `json`, scope and purpose-06 sidecar binding; only the extension field differs. The expected hashes below use the complete canonical raw renderer, exact decoded protection scalar selector, and derived protection-record hash. The independent verifier checks all bytes and hashes.

### 11.3 Signed broker authority and lifecycle known-answer fixture

This **historical V23 one-member codec fixture** uses domain `d`, component `pub`, topic `d-events`, active consumer `consumer`, revision 7, the current domain RegistryFingerprint below, and one `DurableDedup` provider `dedup` on backend `store-a`. The test probe names a valid ULID-shaped MessageId, records two identical delivery attempts across a simulated restart and one committed effect. The fixture broker authority uses the P-256 test private scalar 2 and `broker-test-key` for purposes 08, 09 and 0a; its SPKI below is distinct from V17's event-trust key. The test authority trusts only purposes 08..0a from `2025-01-01T00:00:00Z` through `2030-01-01T00:00:00Z` and is not revoked. Claim issued UTC ticks are `639028224000000000` and expiry ticks are `639029088000000000`; the fence expires at that earliest lease/membership bound. These are test-only fixed bytes and a V1 probe framing example, not production authority configuration or a production-readiness probe; §7 requires the crash/two-route V3 receipt and independent provider query. Python `cryptography` generated the fixed P1363 signatures; the independent Node verifier below parses every record and carrier, recomputes hashes/linkage, checks scope/fingerprint/expiry and rejects one-byte signed-input tampering. An omitted `converters` field and its explicit `[]` default expand to identical V17 canonical options bytes and the unchanged V17 OptionsHash; the three literal V23 option values below exercise the rule. Existing V17/V20/V22 literals above are unchanged.

Run this standalone verifier from the repository root; it reads the literal lines above and checks exact tags, lengths, field order, no trailing bytes, member/config/probe hashes, domain fingerprint, lease/fence expiry, purpose-specific fixed-key signatures and tamper rejection:

```bash
node <<'NODE'
const fs = require('node:fs');
const crypto = require('node:crypto');
const md = fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2] || '_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-event-versioning-upcasting.md', 'utf8');
function literal(name) {
  const hit = md.match(new RegExp('^V23' + name + '=([0-9a-f]+)$', 'm'));
  if (!hit) throw Error('missing literal ' + name);
  return Buffer.from(hit[1], 'hex');
}
function eq(a, b, why) { if (!Buffer.from(a).equals(Buffer.from(b))) throw Error(why); }
function yes(v, why) { if (!v) throw Error(why); }
function sha(x) { return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(x).digest(); }
function u(x) { const b = Buffer.from(x, 'utf8'); const n = Buffer.alloc(4); n.writeUInt32BE(b.length); return Buffer.concat([n,b]); }
function bytes(x) { const n = Buffer.alloc(4); n.writeUInt32BE(x.length); return Buffer.concat([n,x]); }
function parse(buf, separator, types) {
  let pos = 0;
  function take(n) { if (n < 0 || pos + n > buf.length) throw Error('truncated'); const x = buf.subarray(pos,pos+n); pos += n; return x; }
  function value(type) {
    if (type === 'U' || type === 'B') { const n=take(4).readUInt32BE(0); const x=take(n); if(type==='U') { const t=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(x); eq(Buffer.from(t),x,'noncanonical UTF-8'); return t; } return x; }
    if (type === 'H') return take(32);
    if (type === 'N' || type === 'Q') return take(8).readBigInt64BE(0);
    if (type === 'I') return take(4).readInt32BE(0);
    throw Error('type');
  }
  if (separator) { eq(take(Buffer.byteLength(separator)+1),Buffer.from(separator+'\0'),'separator'); yes(take(1)[0]===1,'codec'); yes(take(2).readUInt16BE(0)===types.length,'count'); }
  const out=[];
  for (let j=0;j<types.length;j++) { yes(take(1)[0]===j+1,'tag/order'); out.push(value(types[j])); }
  yes(pos===buf.length,'trailing bytes');
  return out;
}
function carrier(name, claim, purpose, key) {
  const raw=literal(name+'Carrier');
  let p=0;
  function part(n) { yes(p+n<=raw.length,'carrier truncation'); const x=raw.subarray(p,p+n); p+=n; return x; }
  function blob() { return part(part(4).readUInt32BE(0)); }
  const got=blob(); eq(got,claim,'carrier claim');
  const kid=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(blob()); yes(kid==='broker-test-key','key ID');
  const sig=blob(); yes(sig.length===64,'signature length'); yes(p===raw.length,'carrier trailing'); eq(sig,literal(name+'Signature'),'signature literal');
  const signed=Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('HX-EV-SIG-1\0'),Buffer.from([1,purpose,1]),u(kid),Buffer.from([2]),bytes(claim)]);
  yes(crypto.verify('sha256',signed,{key,dsaEncoding:'ieee-p1363'},sig),'signature');
  const tampered=Buffer.from(signed); tampered[tampered.length-1]^=1;
  yes(!crypto.verify('sha256',tampered,{key,dsaEncoding:'ieee-p1363'},sig),'one-byte tamper');
}
const spki=literal('AuthoritySpki');
const key=crypto.createPublicKey({key:spki,format:'der',type:'spki'});
const eventSpki=Buffer.from(md.match(/^V17Spki=([0-9a-f]+)$/m)[1],'hex');
yes(!spki.equals(eventSpki),'broker authority must differ from event key');
const fp=literal('RegistryFingerprint');
const cap=literal('CapabilityHash');
const probe=parse(literal('ProbeReceipt'),'HX-EV-DEDUP-PROBE-1',['U','U','U','H','I','H','U','N','I']);
yes(probe[0]==='d' && probe[1]==='consumer' && probe[2]==='01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FAV','probe scope');
yes(probe[4]===2 && probe[6]==='store-a' && probe[7]===7n && probe[8]===1,'probe two attempts/one effect');
eq(probe[3],literal('DeliveryDigest'),'probe delivery digest literal');
eq(probe[5],literal('EffectReceiptDigest'),'probe effect receipt digest literal');
eq(sha(literal('ProbeReceipt')),literal('ProbeReceiptHash'),'probe hash');
const config=parse(literal('ConsumerConfiguration'),'HX-EV-CONSUMER-CONFIG-1',['U','U','U','U','U','U','U','U','H','U','U','H','U','N']);
yes(config[0]==='consumer' && config[1]==='d' && config[2]==='pub' && config[3]==='d-events' && config[13]===7n,'config scope/revision');
eq(config[8],cap,'capability'); eq(config[11],literal('ProbeReceiptHash'),'probe linkage');
yes(config[9]==='DurableDedup' && config[10]==='dedup' && config[12]==='store-a','dedup provider');
eq(sha(literal('ConsumerConfiguration')),literal('ConsumerConfigurationHash'),'config hash');
const members=literal('MemberSet');
let mpos=0; yes(members.readUInt32BE(mpos)===1,'one active member'); mpos+=4;
const mlen=members.readUInt32BE(mpos); mpos+=4;
yes(members.subarray(mpos,mpos+mlen).toString('utf8')==='consumer','member identity'); mpos+=mlen;
eq(members.subarray(mpos,mpos+32),literal('ConsumerConfigurationHash'),'member config hash'); mpos+=32;
yes(mpos===members.length,'member trailing');
const membership=parse(literal('MembershipClaim'),'HX-EV-MEMBERSHIP-1',['U','U','U','N','H','B','Q','Q']);
yes(membership[0]==='d' && membership[1]==='pub' && membership[2]==='d-events' && membership[3]===7n,'membership scope/revision');
eq(membership[4],fp,'membership fingerprint'); eq(membership[5],members,'membership set');
yes(membership[6]<membership[7],'membership expiry');
const epoch=621355968000000000n;
const validFrom=epoch+BigInt(Date.parse('2025-01-01T00:00:00Z'))*10000n;
const validTo=epoch+BigInt(Date.parse('2030-01-01T00:00:00Z'))*10000n;
yes(membership[6]>=validFrom && membership[7]<=validTo,'authority key interval');
eq(sha(literal('MembershipClaim')),literal('MembershipClaimHash'),'membership hash');
const lease=parse(literal('ConsumerLeaseClaim'),'HX-EV-CONSUMER-LEASE-1',['U','U','U','U','N','H','H','H','H','U','U','H','U','Q','Q']);
yes(lease[0]==='consumer' && lease[1]===membership[0] && lease[2]===membership[1] && lease[3]===membership[2] && lease[4]===membership[3],'lease scope/revision');
eq(lease[5],literal('MembershipClaimHash'),'lease membership hash'); eq(lease[6],literal('ConsumerConfigurationHash'),'lease config hash');
eq(lease[7],fp,'lease fingerprint'); eq(lease[8],cap,'lease capability'); eq(lease[11],literal('ProbeReceiptHash'),'lease probe');
yes(lease[9]==='DurableDedup' && lease[10]==='dedup' && lease[12]==='store-a','lease provider');
yes(lease[13]>=membership[6] && lease[14]<=membership[7] && lease[13]<lease[14],'lease expiry');
eq(sha(literal('ConsumerLeaseClaim')),literal('ConsumerLeaseClaimHash'),'lease hash');
const fence=parse(literal('PublicationFenceClaim'),'HX-EV-PUBLICATION-FENCE-1',['U','U','U','N','H','H','U','Q','B']);
yes(fence[0]===membership[0] && fence[1]===membership[1] && fence[2]===membership[2] && fence[3]===membership[3] && fence[6]==='op','fence scope/revision');
eq(fence[4],literal('MembershipClaimHash'),'fence membership hash'); eq(fence[5],fp,'fence fingerprint');
yes(fence[7]<=lease[14] && fence[7]<=membership[7] && fence[8].length===16,'fence expiry/nonce');
eq(sha(literal('PublicationFenceClaim')),literal('PublicationFenceClaimHash'),'fence hash');
carrier('Membership',literal('MembershipClaim'),8,key);
carrier('ConsumerLease',literal('ConsumerLeaseClaim'),9,key);
carrier('PublicationFence',literal('PublicationFenceClaim'),10,key);

// An omitted optional default and an explicit equal value hash the same expanded tree.
const absentText=literal('OptionsAbsentDefault').toString('utf8');
const explicitText=literal('OptionsExplicitDefault').toString('utf8');
const allowed={converters:'array',implementationId:'string',reader:'string',serializer:'string',signer:'string',validator:'string',version:'number'};
function expandOptions(raw) {
  const x=JSON.parse(raw);
  for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(x)) {
    yes(k in allowed,'unknown option');
    yes((Array.isArray(v)?'array':typeof v)===allowed[k],'wrong-typed option');
  }
  if(!('converters' in x)) x.converters=[];
  yes(Object.keys(x).length===7 && Number.isInteger(x.version),'required options');
  return x;
}
function canonicalValue(x) {
  if (typeof x==='string') return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([4]),u(x)]);
  if (typeof x==='number') return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([3]),u(String(x))]);
  if (Array.isArray(x)) {const n=Buffer.alloc(4);n.writeUInt32BE(x.length);return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([5]),n,...x.map(canonicalValue)]);}
  const names=Object.keys(x).sort((a,b)=>Buffer.compare(Buffer.from(a),Buffer.from(b)));
  const n=Buffer.alloc(4);n.writeUInt32BE(names.length);
  return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from([6]),n,...names.flatMap(k=>[Buffer.from([1]),u(k),canonicalValue(x[k])])]);
}
const a=canonicalValue(expandOptions(absentText));
const z=canonicalValue(expandOptions(explicitText));
eq(a,z,'absent/explicit default canonical bytes');
eq(sha(Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('HX-EV-OPTIONS-1\0'),Buffer.from([1]),bytes(a)])),literal('OptionsExpandedHash'),'expanded options hash');
console.log('V23 broker claims, options default, signatures and tamper checks passed');
NODE
```

The Story 6.6 provider tests additionally exercise a lease expiring after publisher precheck but before broker acceptance, revision/fingerprint change, ambiguous acceptance followed by `GetPublicationReceipt`, changed bytes under one operation ID, same-mode exact retry, and canonical Binary-to-structured conversion. They inspect durable receipt, pin and effect state and assert that an unresolved broker query sends no new nonce or bytes. Scratch, encrypted spool and response-blob cleanup/crash/access tests inspect ownership, readback and deletion without printing payloads.

Review-34 production vectors complete the closure: deliver one authenticated binary body to a physical subscription with two addressed logical HandlerRouteIds, crash after the first route receipt, and prove the broker remains unacknowledged until the second route reaches durable Completed or its authenticated out-of-scope Filtered result. Redeliver after each crash and verify one effect per route, exact manifest/route-set equality and no second effect; change a route ID, pin byte or membership revision and assert conflict without acknowledgement. Exercise `ProjectVerifiedAsync` against Completed with valid/absent state, AlreadyCompleted with present/missing durable receipt, Retryable, Indeterminate, Failed and absent route; assert exactly the §3 response or typed HTTP status and no unearned checkpoint. Drive handler and `SharedProjectionEpochCoordinator` through the same `IVersionedNamedProjectionBatchWriter` with correct and forged capability, wrong key-space, stale root, stale fence and mismatched six-field legacy scope; only a certified pointer exposes rows. Crash after actor save and before publication, then after publication and before public-response acknowledgement; the append result remains byte-identical, a `PublishFailed` public outcome never overwrites it, and exact retry returns only the truthful pinned/reconciled command outcome. Test zero-event no-op under the same boundaries.

For V1, admit a newly written event with branch-01 actor-save certificate and **no** offline manifest, and a retained event with branch-02 independently signed manifest/docket/sidecar; swap branch or event key and assert `LegacyEvidenceConflict` without changing raw bytes. Change one purpose-0d/0e SPKI, interval, revocation or signer/reviewer identity-policy byte and assert `MigrationAuthorityDigest` parity hold while the historical V17 signed claim bytes remain unchanged. At 1,000,000 finite leaves update one key and verify only its expiry job is registered; race stale-root TTL rebase with replacement, saturate 1 GiB job index and 1 GiB hot ordinal log, seal 64 MiB segments and replay across a 64 GiB archive cap with no skipped ordinal or early segment deletion. Issue 100,000 query capabilities and exhaust the 128 MiB tenant index; reject the next issuance, then expire/delete one under ambiguous CAS and prove quota is released only after exact readback. Parse memberships at 1 MiB/1 MiB+1, 4,096/4,097 members and 4 KiB/member; parse external route sets at 256 KiB/256 KiB+1 and 1,024/1,025 rows, and whole carriers at 2 MiB/2 MiB+1 before nested allocation. Commit a multi-event actor batch once, verify every per-member inclusion path against its certified root on publish/duplicate without rereading sibling events, tamper one sidecar/result/sibling/count and assert hold; an exact append retry still requires full-batch equality. Preserve all literal V17/V20/V22/V23 fixture inputs, hashes and signatures and the six-field UNAPPROVED receipt. These vectors inspect durable state, hashes, actor certificates, broker acknowledgements, route receipts and public bytes, not only an HTTP result.

## 12. Content-bound human approval gate

This entire document from its first byte through the newline immediately **before** the unique full-line receipt marker below is normative, including this section, field labels, exact authorization sentence and hash rule. Use UTF-8 without BOM and LF line endings. Compute SHA-256 over those exact bytes; a changed normative byte invalidates approval. The receipt marker line and all six receipt lines after it are mutable and excluded. The only permitted receipt is six single-line `Label: value` fields in this exact order, with no extra approval-bearing rows: `ApprovalDigest`, `Approver`, `ApprovalDateUtc`, `ApprovalScope`, `Authorization`, `ApprovalEvidence`. A completed receipt requires a 64-character lowercase hex `ApprovalDigest` equal to the recomputed hash; a named authenticated human `Approver`; `ApprovalDateUtc` as ISO-8601 UTC ending `Z` **equal to the independently verified source creation/submission UTC**; `ApprovalScope` exactly **`Story 6.5 AD-13 normative artifact`**; `Authorization` exactly **`I approve this exact Story 6.5 normative digest and explicitly authorize Story 6.6 implementation.`**; and `ApprovalEvidence` naming a content-addressed immutable authenticated capture of the exact human-authored body, actor identity, source ID, source creation/submission UTC, digest and authorization sentence. A verified signed commit or append-only evidence record is admissible when its exact bytes, immutable address, actor and time can be independently authenticated. A GitHub review/comment URL alone is editable and insufficient: the validator independently fetches the live source, compares it to the immutable capture, and rechecks edit, deletion and revocation status before accepting the receipt. Capture time supplied by an assistant does not establish `ApprovalDateUtc`. An edit, missing/changed capture, deleted/revoked source, conditional text, changed labels, extra fields, stale digest or unauthenticated actor leaves Story 6.5 in progress and Story 6.6 unauthorized. The unapproved receipt uses `UNAPPROVED` for digest, approver, date, authorization and evidence, while keeping the fixed scope. `epics.md` may say in progress before approval; mark 6.5 done or authorize 6.6 only after this full gate verifies.

Exact recomputation from repository root (the printed value is **not** approval):

```bash
python3 -c 'from pathlib import Path; import hashlib; p=Path("_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-event-versioning-upcasting.md").read_bytes(); m=b"<!-- APPROVAL RECEIPT: mutable fields below -->\n"; lines=p.splitlines(keepends=True); assert sum(x==m for x in lines)==1 and not p.startswith(b"\xef\xbb\xbf") and b"\r" not in p; print(hashlib.sha256(p[:sum(map(len, lines[:lines.index(m)]))]).hexdigest())'
```

<!-- APPROVAL RECEIPT: mutable fields below -->
ApprovalDigest: UNAPPROVED
Approver: UNAPPROVED
ApprovalDateUtc: UNAPPROVED
ApprovalScope: Story 6.5 AD-13 normative artifact
Authorization: UNAPPROVED
ApprovalEvidence: UNAPPROVED
